# a-unit-check-runs-the-importers-too

- State: approved
- Priority: 1
- Sequence: 30
- Risk: severity=1 novelty=1 exposure=2 accumulation=1 basis="Test selection inside the unit check only; a wrong selection costs check time or a static-only fallback, never a landing; the lane's full proof stays the net; the 50 percent cap is unchanged"
- Tier: 1
- Intent: A unit check runs whole every internal package that reaches a changed package through production imports, keeps test imports as a last hop only, narrows to the direct importers when the selection is capped instead of falling to a static-only check, selects a source audit by the path it reads, and runs named cmd/metasystem tests by name (finding 429; design plans/designs/the-stewards-act-on-what-they-see.md (accepted, revision 4) D2, D3, G2)
- Origin: main
- Next step: On ui after the-impact-check-selects-only-tests-this-host-can-run lands (same files): opus-designer cuts the brief from the page (BRIEF-CHECKLIST, Read each round: yes, one Astra round, the reverse-dependent packages run whole); one unit, about 60 production lines: landing/batch/goadapter/unitgate.go, impact.go, cmd/metasystem/test_impact.go; reverses part of 4e690dba7f (decision 9.1 of the page) Decision 212 in Wido name (OPEN 1 of the fixes analysis): a production change in cmd/metasystem runs the package whole at the seat and is exempt from the static-only fallback (test_impact.go:249-260, :367). Reverses the named-selection part of D2/D3 of the-stewards-act-on-what-they-see. Acceptance: the check log shows TestDeclarationInversePublicRevert/inherited-mixed executed. About 25 lines. Rejected: a call-graph selector. Week review (Astra r2 finding 7): the prerequisite the-impact-check-selects-only-tests-this-host-can-run is done (e1c2781182); the intent keeps its accepted D2/D3 work (production-import closure, test imports as terminal edges, direct-importer fallback, source-audit selection) and adds decision 212 (a production change in cmd/metasystem runs the package whole, exempt from the static-only fallback test_impact.go:249-260). Inventory and size the combined obligations in the brief; split if over 125 counted lines. Decision 224 (2026-11-07): decision 212 amended: seat and resolution checks are targeted (named selections inside cmd/metasystem too); the shared cheapness change at test_impact.go:367 is owned by the-lane-batches-goals-and-proves-once U3; this goal builds the shared selector on it and drops "runs the package whole". Struggle program item 3 (2026-11-10, /Users/wido/agentic-tools-evidence/lane-test-20261009/designs/struggle-program-2026-11-10.md, supersedes any whole-command-package prescription): U-files (120-180): contract groups may select test FILES that are required when their inputs change; initial maps internal/landing/plain/** -> cmd/metasystem/landing_red_permission_test.go and landing_pipeline_test.go, internal/launch/** -> cmd/metasystem/intent_tree*_test.go; expanded to package-qualified top-level tests from the candidate. U-required (70-110): required files survive group dropping and static fallback (cmd/metasystem/test_impact.go:184,249; internal/landing/batch/goadapter/impact.go:133). Its dependency on batching U3b is met (on main). Finding 599 (2026-11-11): source-scanning audit tests (message audit, wall-time audit, seam allow-list) are required whenever a production .go file under their scan root changes; verify through the frozen-export execution path the unit check uses. Finding 601 stays with the scratch-test goal (fixture/environment), not the selector.
